     1	/**
     2	 * Room Response Simulation Benchmark
     3	 * 
     4	 * This is a distributed-memory implementation of room impulse response simulation
     5	 * using radiosity-based wave propagation. It models how sound/light
     6	 * waves propagate between surfaces in a room by computing:
     7	 * 
     8	 * 1. Form factors (Kij) between all pairs of triangles based on visibility and geometry
     9	 * 2. Wave propagation using radiosity equations with time delays (Tau)
    10	 * 3. Distance estimation via cross-correlation of radiosity values
    11	 * 
    12	 * The implementation generates a procedural icosphere mesh to simulate a room.
    13	 */
    14	
    15	#include <algorithm>
    16	#include <array>
    17	#include <cmath>
    18	#include <cstdint>
    19	#include <cstdio>
    20	#include <cstdlib>
    21	#include <cstring>
    22	#include <limits>
    23	#include <map>
    24	#include <memory>
    25	#include <mpi.h>
    26	#include <random>
    27	#include <vector>
    28	
    29	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    30	
    31	// ============================================================================
    32	// Types and Constants
    33	// ============================================================================
    34	
    35	using idx_t = uint32_t;
    36	using val_t = float;
    37	
    38	constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
    39	constexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;
    40	constexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;
    41	constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
    42	constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;
    43	constexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling
    44	constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
    45	constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
    46	
    47	// ============================================================================
    48	// Vector and Triangle Types
    49	// ============================================================================
    50	
    51	struct Vec3 {
    52	    val_t x, y, z;
    53	
    54	    constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}
    55	    constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    56	    explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}
    57	
    58	    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
    59	    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
    60	    Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }
   450	// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
   451	// ============================================================================
   452	
   453	// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
   454	// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
   455	bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
   456	                  const Octree& octree,
   457	                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
   458	    Vec3 dir = to - from;
   459	    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
   460	    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
   461	    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;
   462	
   463	    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
   464	        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;
   465	
   466	        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
   467	        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
   468	            return true;  // Ray is blocked
   469	        }
   470	        return false;
   471	    });
   472	}
   473	
   474	// ============================================================================
   475	// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
   476	// ============================================================================
   477	
   478	// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal
   479	val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
   480	    val_t vNorm = v.norm();
   481	    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;
   482	    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);
   483	}
   484	
   485	// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
   486	val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
   487	                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
   488	                  const Octree& octree,
   489	                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
   490	    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
   491	    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];
   492	
   493	    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
   494	    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;
   495	
   496	    val_t kij = ZERO;
   497	
   498	    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
   499	        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
   500	        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);
   501	
   502	        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;
   503	
   504	        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
   505	        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
   506	        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
   507	
   508	        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());
   509	        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());
   510	
   511	        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;
   512	
   513	        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
   514	    }
   515	
   516	    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
   517	}
   518	
   519	// ============================================================================
   520	// Tau (time delay) Computation
   521	// ============================================================================
   522	
   523	int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
   524	    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
   525	    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
   526	}
   527	
   528	// ============================================================================
   529	// Room Response Simulation State
   530	// ============================================================================
   531	
   532	struct SimulationState {
   533	    size_t numTriangles;
   534	    size_t numTimesteps;
   535	
   536	    int mpiRank;
   537	    int mpiSize;
   538	    size_t localBegin;              // First receiver row owned by this rank
   539	    size_t localCount;              // Number of receiver rows owned by this rank
   540	    std::vector<int> rowCounts;     // MPI counts for one global radiosity slice
   541	    std::vector<int> rowDispls;
   542	
   543	    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
   544	    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
   545	    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
   546	    std::vector<val_t> coupling;    // Local min(Kij * area[j], 1) rows
   547	    std::vector<uint8_t> tau;       // Local time-delay rows
   548	    std::vector<val_t> radWindow;   // Replicated ring of recent global radiosity
   549	    std::vector<val_t> localRadB;   // Local receiver histories (T x localCount)
   550	    std::vector<val_t> sourceRadB;  // Complete source history for correlation
   551	    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Complete on rank zero after MPI_Gatherv
   552	
   553	    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure
   554	
   555	    size_t sourceIndex;
   556	    size_t windowSize = 1;
   557	    uint64_t localNonZeroKij = 0;
   558	    uint64_t globalNonZeroKij = 0;
   559	    uint64_t globalReceivedEnergy = 0;
   560	
   561	    size_t idxLocal(size_t localI, size_t j) const { return localI * numTriangles + j; }
   562	    size_t idxWindow(size_t t, size_t n) const { return (t % windowSize) * numTriangles + n; }
   563	    size_t idxLocalTime(size_t t, size_t localI) const { return t * localCount + localI; }
   564	};
   565	
   566	// ============================================================================
   567	// Initialization
   568	// ============================================================================
   569	
   570	void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
   571	                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
   572	    // Generate mesh
   573	    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
   574	    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
   575	    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
   576	    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
   577	    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
   578	
   579	    const size_t baseRows = state.numTriangles / static_cast<size_t>(state.mpiSize);
   580	    const size_t extraRows = state.numTriangles % static_cast<size_t>(state.mpiSize);
   581	    state.localCount = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(state.mpiRank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
   582	    state.localBegin = static_cast<size_t>(state.mpiRank) * baseRows +
   583	                       std::min(static_cast<size_t>(state.mpiRank), extraRows);
   584	
   585	    state.rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(state.mpiSize));
   586	    state.rowDispls.resize(static_cast<size_t>(state.mpiSize));
   587	    for (int rank = 0; rank < state.mpiSize; ++rank) {
   588	        const size_t rankRows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
   589	        const size_t rankBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
   590	                                 std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraRows);
   591	        state.rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankRows);
   592	        state.rowDispls[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankBegin);
   593	    }
   594	
   595	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) {
   596	        printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
   597	    }
   598	
   599	    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
   600	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) printf("Building octree...\n");
   601	    state.octree.build(state.triangles);
   602	
   603	    // Initialize areas
   604	    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
   605	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   606	        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
   607	    }
   608	
   609	    // Initialize reflectivity
   610	    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);
   611	
   612	    // Only the O(N^2/P) receiver rows assigned to this rank are resident locally.
   613	    state.coupling.resize(state.localCount * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   614	    state.tau.resize(state.localCount * state.numTriangles, 0);
   615	    state.localRadB.resize(timesteps * state.localCount, ZERO);
   616	    state.sourceRadB.resize(timesteps, ZERO);
   617	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   618	}
   619	
   620	// ============================================================================
   621	// Precomputation Phase
   622	// ============================================================================
   623	
   624	void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
   625	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
   626	    RandomGenerator rng(42);
   627	
   628	    // Move to the same point in the original global random stream at which this
   629	    // rank's first row starts. computeKij consumes four samples per ray unless
   630	    // the pair is culled before sampling.
   631	    constexpr size_t samplesPerPair = 4 * NUM_RAYS;
   632	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.localBegin; ++i) {
   633	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   634	            if (i == j) continue;
   635	            if (state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
   636	                rng.skip(samplesPerPair);
   637	            }
   638	        }
   639	    }
   640	
   641	    state.localNonZeroKij = 0;
   642	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localCount; ++localI) {
   643	        const size_t i = state.localBegin + localI;
   644	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   645	            if (i == j) continue;
   646	            const val_t kij = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
   647	            if (kij > EPSILON) ++state.localNonZeroKij;
   648	
   649	            // This product is invariant over all timesteps. Storing it once also
   650	            // cuts work from the innermost propagation loop.
   651	            state.coupling[state.idxLocal(localI, j)] = std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE);
   652	        }
   653	    }
   654	
   655	    unsigned long long localCount = static_cast<unsigned long long>(state.localNonZeroKij);
   656	    unsigned long long globalCount = 0;
   657	    MPI_Reduce(&localCount, &globalCount, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, 0,
   658	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   659	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) {
   660	        state.globalNonZeroKij = static_cast<uint64_t>(globalCount);
   661	        printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
   662	    }
   663	}
   664	
   665	void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
   666	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");
   667	
   668	    int localMaxTau = 0;
   669	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localCount; ++localI) {
   670	        const size_t i = state.localBegin + localI;
   671	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   672	            if (i == j) continue;
   673	            const int delay = computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
   674	            state.tau[state.idxLocal(localI, j)] = static_cast<uint8_t>(delay);
   675	            localMaxTau = std::max(localMaxTau, delay);
   676	        }
   677	    }
   678	
   679	    int globalMaxTau = 0;
   680	    MPI_Allreduce(&localMaxTau, &globalMaxTau, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   681	    state.windowSize = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(globalMaxTau) + 1,
   682	                                std::max(state.numTimesteps, size_t{1}));
   683	    state.radWindow.resize(state.windowSize * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   684	}
   685	
   686	// ============================================================================
   687	// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
   688	// ============================================================================
   689	
   690	void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
   691	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
   692	
   693	    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   694	        val_t* currentRadiosity = state.radWindow.data() + state.idxWindow(t, 0);
   695	
   696	        for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localCount; ++localI) {
   697	            const size_t i = state.localBegin + localI;
   698	            const val_t* const couplingRow =
   699	                state.coupling.data() + state.idxLocal(localI, 0);
   700	            const uint8_t* const tauRow = state.tau.data() + state.idxLocal(localI, 0);
   701	            val_t sumB = ZERO;
   702	
   703	            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   704	                const size_t tauij = tauRow[j];
   705	
   706	                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
   707	                if (t < tauij) continue;
   708	
   709	                const val_t transfer = couplingRow[j];
   710	                if (transfer <= ZERO) continue;
   711	
   712	                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
   713	                const size_t srcTime = t - tauij;
   714	                val_t radJ = state.radWindow[state.idxWindow(srcTime, j)];
   715	                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
   716	
   717	                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
   718	                sumB += transfer * radJ;
   719	            }
   720	
   721	            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
   722	            const val_t emission =
   723	                (i == state.sourceIndex && t < state.numTimesteps / 2) ? ONE : ZERO;
   724	            currentRadiosity[i] = state.rho[i] * sumB + emission;
   725	            state.localRadB[state.idxLocalTime(t, localI)] = currentRadiosity[i];
   726	        }
   727	
   728	        // Every row at this timestep is independent because all non-diagonal
   729	        // delays are positive. Exchange just the completed O(N) slice.
   730	        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_FLOAT, currentRadiosity,
   731	                       state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDispls.data(), MPI_FLOAT,
   732	                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   733	        state.sourceRadB[t] = currentRadiosity[state.sourceIndex];
   734	
   735	        if (state.mpiRank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {
   736	            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
   737	        }
   738	    }
   739	
   740	    uint64_t localReceivedEnergy = 0;
   741	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localCount; ++localI) {
   742	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   743	            if (state.localRadB[state.idxLocalTime(t, localI)] > EPSILON) {
   744	                ++localReceivedEnergy;
   745	                break;
   746	            }
   747	        }
   748	    }
   749	    unsigned long long localCount = static_cast<unsigned long long>(localReceivedEnergy);
   750	    unsigned long long globalCount = 0;
   751	    MPI_Reduce(&localCount, &globalCount, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, 0,
   752	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   753	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) {
   754	        state.globalReceivedEnergy = static_cast<uint64_t>(globalCount);
   755	    }
   756	}
   757	
   758	// ============================================================================
   759	// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
   760	// ============================================================================
   761	
   762	void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
   763	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
   764	    std::vector<val_t> localDistances(state.localCount, ZERO);
   765	
   766	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localCount; ++localI) {
   767	        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
   768	        int bestT = 0;
   769	
   770	        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
   771	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   772	            val_t sum = ZERO;
   773	
   774	            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
   775	                val_t pB = state.localRadB[state.idxLocalTime(tt, localI)];
   776	                val_t pS = state.sourceRadB[tt - t];
   777	                sum += pS * pB;
   778	            }
   779	
   780	            if (sum > maxCorr) {
   800	    printf("\nValidation:\n");
   801	
   802	    // Check that distances are non-negative
   803	    bool allNonNegative = true;
   804	    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   805	    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   806	    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
   807	    int nonZeroCount = 0;
   808	
   809	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   810	        val_t d = state.distances[i];
   811	        if (d < 0) {
   812	            allNonNegative = false;
   813	            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   814	        }
   815	        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
   816	            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   817	            return false;
   818	        }
   819	        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
   820	        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
   821	        sumDist += d;
   822	        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
   823	    }
   824	
   825	    printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
   826	    printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
   827	    printf("  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);
   828	
   829	    // Check source distance is zero or very small
   830	    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
   831	    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
   832	        printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
   833	    }
   834	
   835	    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)
   836	    const uint64_t receivedEnergy = state.globalReceivedEnergy;
   837	
   838	    printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %" PRIu64 "/%zu\n",
   839	           receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);
   840	
   841	    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
   842	        printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
   843	        return false;
   844	    }
   845	
   846	    // Check the globally reduced count of non-zero distributed Kij rows.
   847	    const uint64_t nonZeroKij = state.globalNonZeroKij;
   848	    printf("  Non-zero form factors: %" PRIu64 "/%zu (%.2f%%)\n",
   849	           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,
   850	           100.0f * static_cast<val_t>(nonZeroKij) /
   851	               static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));
   852	
   853	    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {
   854	        printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
   855	        return false;
   856	    }
   857	
   858	    if (!allNonNegative) {
   859	        return false;
   860	    }
   861	
   862	    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
   863	    return true;
   864	}
   865	
   866	// ============================================================================
   867	// Hash for Verification
   868	// ============================================================================
   869	
   870	uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
   871	    uint64_t hash = 0;
   872	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   873	        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
   874	        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   875	    }
   876	    return hash;
   877	}
   878	
   879	// ============================================================================
   880	// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
   881	// ============================================================================
   882	
   883	int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
   884	    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
   885	    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
   886	    int subdivisions = 0;
   887	    int triangles = 20;
   888	    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
   889	        subdivisions++;
   890	        triangles *= 4;
   891	    }
   892	    return subdivisions;
   893	}
   894	
   895	// ============================================================================
   896	// Main
   897	// ============================================================================
   898	
   899	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   900	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   901	    printf("Options:\n");
   902	    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
   903	    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
   904	    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
   905	    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
   906	    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
   907	    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
   908	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   909	    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
   910	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   911	}
   912	
   913	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   914	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   915	
   916	    int mpiRank = 0;
   917	    int mpiSize = 1;
   918	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &mpiRank);
   919	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &mpiSize);
   920	
   921	    int targetTriangles = 320;
   922	    int timesteps = 50;
   923	    int sourceIdx = 0;
   924	    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
   925	    bool validate = false;
   926	    bool printResults = false;
   927	
   928	    // Parse command line arguments
   929	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   930	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   931	            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
   932	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   933	            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   934	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   935	            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
   936	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   937	            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
   938	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   939	            validate = true;
   940	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
   941	            printResults = true;
   942	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   943	            if (mpiRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
   944	            MPI_Finalize();
   945	            return 0;
   946	        } else {
   947	            if (mpiRank == 0) {
   948	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   949	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   950	            }
   951	            MPI_Finalize();
   952	            return 1;
   953	        }
   954	    }
   955	
   956	    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);
   957	
   958	    if (mpiRank == 0) {
   959	        printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
   960	        printf("===================================\n");
   961	        printf("MPI processes: %d\n", mpiSize);
   962	        printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
   963	        printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
   964	        printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
   965	        printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
   966	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   967	        printf("\n");
   968	    }
   969	
   970	    // Initialize
   971	    SimulationState state;
   972	    state.mpiRank = mpiRank;
   973	    state.mpiSize = mpiSize;
   974	    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
   975	                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);
   976	
   977	    if (mpiRank == 0) printf("\n");
   978	
   979	    // Precomputation
   980	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   981	    double startPre = MPI_Wtime();
   982	
   983	    computeTimeDelays(state);
   984	    computeFormFactors(state);
   985	
   986	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   987	    double localPreDuration = MPI_Wtime() - startPre;
   988	    double preSeconds = 0.0;
   989	    MPI_Reduce(&localPreDuration, &preSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   990	    long preDuration = static_cast<long>(preSeconds * 1000.0);
   991	
   992	    if (mpiRank == 0) {
   993	        printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
   994	        printf("\n");
   995	    }
   996	
   997	    // Simulation
   998	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   999	    double startSim = MPI_Wtime();
  1000	
  1001	    runSimulation(state);
  1002	
  1003	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  1004	    double localSimDuration = MPI_Wtime() - startSim;
  1005	    double simSeconds = 0.0;
  1006	    MPI_Reduce(&localSimDuration, &simSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  1007	    long simDuration = static_cast<long>(simSeconds * 1000.0);
  1008	
  1009	    if (mpiRank == 0) {
  1010	        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
  1011	        printf("\n");
  1012	    }
  1013	
  1014	    // Distance computation
  1015	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  1016	    double startDist = MPI_Wtime();
  1017	
  1018	    computeDistances(state);
  1019	
  1020	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  1021	    double localDistDuration = MPI_Wtime() - startDist;
  1022	    double distSeconds = 0.0;
  1023	    MPI_Reduce(&localDistDuration, &distSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  1024	    long distDuration = static_cast<long>(distSeconds * 1000.0);
  1025	
  1026	    if (mpiRank == 0) {
  1027	        printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
  1028	        printf("\n");
  1029	    }
  1030	
  1031	    // Total time
  1032	    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
  1033	    int exitCode = 0;
  1034	    if (mpiRank == 0) {
  1035	        printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);
  1036	
  1037	        // Performance metrics
  1038	        size_t n = state.numTriangles;
  1039	        size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
  1040	        double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(n);
  1041	        double simOps = kijOps * static_cast<double>(t);
  1042	        double distOps = static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(t) *
  1043	                         static_cast<double>(t);
  1044	
  1045	        printf("\nPerformance:\n");
  1046	        printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
  1047	        printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
  1048	        printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
  1049	        printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
  1050	        printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
  1051	        printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);
  1052	
  1053	        // The quadratic matrices are distributed; the time history is replicated
  1054	        // because every receiver can depend on any emitter at an earlier timestep.
  1055	        size_t memCoupling = state.localCount * n * sizeof(val_t);
  1056	        size_t memTau = state.localCount * n * sizeof(uint8_t);
  1057	        size_t memRad = (state.windowSize * n + t * state.localCount + t) * sizeof(val_t);
  1058	        size_t totalMem = memCoupling + memTau + memRad;
  1059	        printf("  Memory usage per rank (rank 0): %.2f MB\n",
  1060	               totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
     val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+
+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
+    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI
+    // process count.
+    void skip(size_t samples) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < samples; ++i) {
+            (void)dist(rng);
+        }
+    }

   390	// ============================================================================
   391	// Random Number Generation
   392	// ============================================================================
   393	
   394	class RandomGenerator {
   395	    std::mt19937 rng;
   396	    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
   397	public:
   398	    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
   399	    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
   400	
   401	    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
   402	    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI
   403	    // process count.
   404	    void skip(size_t samples) {
   405	        for (size_t i = 0; i < samples; ++i) {
   406	            (void)dist(rng);
   407	        }
   408	    }
   409	};
   410	
   411	// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
   412	Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
   413	    val_t u = rng.rand();
   414	    val_t v = rng.rand();
   415	    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
   416	        u = 1.0f - u;
   417	        v = 1.0f - v;
   418	    }
   419	    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
   420	    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
   421	    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
   422	}
   423	
   424	// ============================================================================
   425	// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
   426	// ============================================================================
   427	
   428	val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
   429	                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
   430	    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
   431	    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
   432	    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
   433	    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);
   434	
   435	    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   436	
   437	    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
   438	    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
   439	    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
   440	    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   441	
   442	    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
   443	    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
   444	    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   445	
   446	    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
   447	}

   450	// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)

   759	// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
   760	// ============================================================================
   761	
   762	void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
   763	    if (state.mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
   764	    std::vector<val_t> localDistances(state.localCount, ZERO);
   765	
   766	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localCount; ++localI) {
   767	        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
   768	        int bestT = 0;
   769	
   770	        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
   771	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   772	            val_t sum = ZERO;
   773	
   774	            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
   775	                val_t pB = state.localRadB[state.idxLocalTime(tt, localI)];
   776	                val_t pS = state.sourceRadB[tt - t];
   777	                sum += pS * pB;
   778	            }
   779	
   780	            if (sum > maxCorr) {
   781	                maxCorr = sum;
   782	                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
   783	            }
   784	        }
   785	
   786	        localDistances[localI] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
   787	    }
   788	
   789	    MPI_Gatherv(localDistances.data(), static_cast<int>(state.localCount), MPI_FLOAT,
   790	                state.mpiRank == 0 ? state.distances.data() : nullptr,
   791	                state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDispls.data(), MPI_FLOAT, 0,
   792	                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   793	}

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
     val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+
+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
+    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI
+    // process count.
+    void skip(size_t samples) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < samples; ++i) {
+            (void)dist(rng);
+        }
+    }

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
     val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+
+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
+    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI
+    // process count.
+    void skip(size_t samples) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < samples; ++i) {
+            (void)dist(rng);
+        }
+    }
 };

+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}

+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
+    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI

+    void skip(size_t samples) {

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
     val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }

+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
+    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI
+    // process count.
+    void skip(size_t samples) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < samples; ++i) {
+            (void)dist(rng);

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}

+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
+    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI
+    // process count.
+    void skip(size_t samples) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < samples; ++i) {

+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
     val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+
+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
+    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI
+    // process count.
+    void skip(size_t samples) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < samples; ++i) {
+            (void)dist(rng);
+        }
+    }

codex
The default 320-triangle workload is bit-for-bit identical at 1 and 4 ranks and passes validation. Its measured compute phases improved from about 1.50 s to 0.38 s for precomputation and 16 ms to 4 ms for propagation in this environment. A 24-rank run on only 20 triangles also matched exactly, confirming zero-row ranks are handled safely; AddressSanitizer/UBSan found no issues in a 2-rank validation run.

+    // Advancing through the distribution (rather than reseeding per rank) preserves
+    // the exact random stream, and consequently the one-rank result, for every MPI
+    // process count.
+    void skip(size_t samples) {

codex
Implemented unconditional MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `roomsim` executable name.

Key changes:

- Distributed receiver rows across MPI ranks, reducing quadratic storage to \(O(N^2/P)\).
- Added timestep `MPI_Allgatherv` synchronization.
- Distributed radiosity histories with a bounded replicated delay window.
- Preserved the original random stream and floating-point reduction order, producing identical results across rank counts.
- Restricted reporting and validation output to rank 0.
- Updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r3/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:8) to require MPI.
- Modified only [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r3/roomsim/roomsim.cpp:532) and `CMakeLists.txt`.

Verification completed:

- Clean CMake build succeeded.
- 1, 2, 3, 4, and 24-rank results matched exactly.
- External result blocks were byte-for-byte identical to the original.
- Default validation passed.
- AddressSanitizer and UBSan passed.
- Default precomputation scaled from approximately 1.50 s on one rank to 0.38 s on four ranks.

Run with:

```bash
cmake -S roomsim -B build
cmake --build build -j
mpirun -np 4 build/roomsim -n 320 -t 50 -v
```

tokens used
109,036
Implemented unconditional MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `roomsim` executable name.

Key changes:

- Distributed receiver rows across MPI ranks, reducing quadratic storage to \(O(N^2/P)\).
- Added timestep `MPI_Allgatherv` synchronization.
- Distributed radiosity histories with a bounded replicated delay window.
- Preserved the original random stream and floating-point reduction order, producing identical results across rank counts.
- Restricted reporting and validation output to rank 0.
- Updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r3/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:8) to require MPI.
- Modified only [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r3/roomsim/roomsim.cpp:532) and `CMakeLists.txt`.

Verification completed:

- Clean CMake build succeeded.
- 1, 2, 3, 4, and 24-rank results matched exactly.
- External result blocks were byte-for-byte identical to the original.
- Default validation passed.
- AddressSanitizer and UBSan passed.
- Default precomputation scaled from approximately 1.50 s on one rank to 0.38 s on four ranks.

Run with:

```bash
cmake -S roomsim -B build
cmake --build build -j
mpirun -np 4 build/roomsim -n 320 -t 50 -v
```
